Let's detail LSE function:
```
def lse_z_value_grad(z, beta):
    a = softmax_z(z) # sum1
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cmax = c.max()
    sh = beta*(c-cmax); np.clip; e=exp; L=cmax+np.log(e.sum())/beta
    p=e/e.sum()
    v = weighted_deriv(p, a)
    g_a = 4.0*n*v
    dot = np.dot(a,g_a)
    g_z = a*(g_a - dot)
    f = 2.0*n*L
    return f, g_z
```


Soft-z to a:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: e=np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
    return e/s
```

Implementation of box projection with sum1:
```
def project_conv_to_box(c, cap):
    q = c.copy()
    # If cap*len <1, set cap=1/len
    if cap < 1.0/len(q): cap = 1.0/len(q)*(1+1e-12)
    # Ensure nonnegative
    q[q<0]=0
    # Iteratively water fill: reduce over cap, distribute deficit to under cap
    for _ in range(5):
       over = q > cap
       if not np.any(over): break
       excess = (q[over]-cap).sum()
       q[over]=cap
       under = q < cap
       if under.sum() == 0: break
       q[under] += excess / under.sum()
    # If sum q <1 (if blanket? Actually clipping reduces sum; we want increase low. The above just conserves sum? No, clipping reduces sum; adding excess to under restores original sum, not 1? Wait c sum should normalize to1 first. If sum original=1, after clipping and redistributing excess, sum remains1. That is good: we preserve sum=1 by moving excess from peaks to troughs only, not capping and scaling. This pushes conv to box while keeping sum. But if q has over cap and no under? impossible if cap avg.
       # Some under may exceed cap after bulk add; loop.
    # After loop, due precision, maybe few over; set q[over]=cap and adjust? If sum not 1 after adding? It should, but due finite. If q sum not 1, scale then loop.
    s=q.sum()
    if not close(s,1): q = q/s; loop again.
    return q
```
But if c has many values above cap and few below, adding equal excess to all under may raise many above, loop handles. If all under cap and sum=1, no change. To force lower cap, need cap below current max; there will be over. This moves mass from over to under uniformly. This is a projection onto box with fixed sum? It equalizes under values, maybe too blunt. Alternative water-fill: raise lowest under first until all cap or sum restored. Equal adding is okay but may over-accumulate. We'll do iterative.

Then q_pad zero outside. FFT Q. sqrt branch:
```
def sqrt_fft_hermitian(Q):
    N=len(Q)
    A=np.zeros(N, complex)
    half=N//2
    # principal sqrt of Q[0:half+1]
    A[:half+1] = np.sqrt(Q[:half+1]) # but np.sqrt of complex okay
    # enforce Hermitian: A[N-k]=conj(A[k])
    for k in range(1,half): A[N-k]=np.conj(A[k])
    if N%2==0:
       # Nyquist must be real; Q[nyq] real maybe; set A[nyq]=np.sqrt(max(0,real(Q[nyq]))) with sign? 
       A[half]=np.sqrt(max(0.0, Q[half].real)) + 0j
    return A
```
But `np.sqrt` of Q half may not preserve Q[N-k] if branch cut; using conj for mirror okay. If Q[k] and Q[N-k] are conjugates, sqrt conj works. Good.
Then a_new = np.fft.ifft(A, N)[:n].real. a_new may have sum not1 or negative. Clip, normalize. If after clipping sum zero, skip.
Maybe to keep tail penalty, if a_new has many small negative due numerical, clipping okay. Could blend `a = (1-lam)*a + lam*a_new`. lam=0.5? If a_new bad, blend protects. We can also only accept if exact score lower after update; otherwise revert. Because map not guaranteed. Use `a_cand` and if score < best, set; else maybe use small blend? The spectral method may temporarily worsen. Algorithm can maintain best but also use current for further mapping; maybe alternating converge even if not monotone. `lse_z_value_grad` details:
```
def lse_z_value_grad(z,beta):
    a = softmax_z(z)
    n=len(a)
    c=conv_fft(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
    sh=beta*(c-cmax); np.clip(sh,-700,700)
    ex=np.exp(sh)
    s=ex.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: ex=np.full_like(c,1.0); s=len(c)
    L=cmax + np.log(s)/beta
    p=ex/s
    v=weighted_v(p,a)
    g_a=4.0*n*v
    g_mean=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
    g_z=a*(g_a - g_mean)
    f=2.0*n*L
    # optionally normalize gradient? no.
    return f,g_z
```
Why g_a=4n v? F=2n L; grad_L_a=2v; so grad_F_a=4n v. For LSE beta, L scale c. Good. The constant 4n might be too large; L-BFGS can handle. Could drop constant n? Since objective scale has n, step size adaptation. Keep for correct.

But z variable: If we subtract mean in softmax, gradient wrt z is a*(g_a - dot(a,g_a)). This gradient is orthogonal to ones? Sum a_i*(...) = dot - sum a*dot =0, yes. Good.

`softmax_z` robust:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if not all finite: z=np.zeros_like(z)
    zmax=np.max(z)
    ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0: ez=np.ones_like(z); s=len(z)
    return ez/s
```
If z has very large range, exp negative underflows to 0, sum maybe 0 if all -inf? zmax finite, at least max exp 1, sum>=1. Good.

Low-dim function objective:
```
def fourier_lse_func(y, base, modes, beta):
    n=len(base)
    u = np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300)) + modes @ y
    a = softmax_z(u)
    c=conv_fft(a); ... same LSE
    # gradient wrt y:
    G = a * (4*n*v - np.dot(a,4*n*v)? Actually grad_a=4n v; use g_a.
    # derivative of softmax(u) wrt y_j: a*(M_j - mean_a(M_j))
    # grad_y_j = dot(g_a, a*(M_j - mean_Mj)) where mean_Mj = dot(a,M_j) = modes.T @ a
    # Let H = g_a*a. grad_y = modes.T @ H - (H·a) * (modes.T @ a)
    H = a*g_a
    hat = float(np.dot(H,a))
    Ma = modes.T @ a  # (s,n) @ (n) -> s
    Mz? offset base? modes.T @ H.
    gy = modes.T @ H - hat*Ma
    return f,gy
```

`project_box`: Input q sum maybe not 1; normalize first? We'll ensure q sum1. Use loop 10:
```
def project_box(q, cap):
    q=q.copy(); q[q<0]=0
    s=q.sum(); if s>0: q=q/s
    cap=max(cap, 1.0/len(q)*(1+1e-12))
    for _ in range(20):
       over = q > cap
       if not np.any(over): break
       over_idx = np.where(over)[0]
       excess = (q[over_idx]-cap).sum()
       q[over_idx] = cap
       under = q < cap - 1e-15
       if not np.any(under):
           # distribute to all
           q += excess/len(q)
       else:
           under_idx=np.where(under)[0]
           inc = excess/under_idx.size
           q[under_idx] += inc
    s=q.sum()
    if s>0: q=q/s
    # If still over cap due scaling, set over and maybe accept small violation? We want cap maybe slightly relaxed.
    return q
```
But after final scaling by s, some over may exceed cap if s differs from 1 due precision; we can not scale if sum close. If sum not 1 after loop (it should), scale then possibly violate. Use cap slightly higher after scaling. Or loop iters after scaling.

```
def spectral_sqrt_update(q,N=None):
```

`next_power_of_two(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
If m=0 bit? handle.

`normalize_sum1(a)`:
```
def normalize_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    a = a/s
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    return a/s
```
`nan_to_num` may turn inf to 0, but then sum maybe zero. okay.

`resample_to_n(a, n)`:
```
def resample_to_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size == n: return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if a.size == 1: return np.full(n,float(a[0]))/? normalize after
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_sum1(b)
```

`softmax_from_z(z)`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.zeros_like(z)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zmax): z=np.zeros_like(z); zmax=0.0
    ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=ez.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        ez=np.ones_like(z); s=float(z.size)
    return ez/s
```
If zmax huge, z-zmax may underflow; okay.

`z_from_a(a)`:
```
def z_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
    z=np.log(a)
    return z - float(np.mean(z))
```

`conv_fft(a)`:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    length=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(length)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:length]
    # small negative due roundoff
    return c
```
For nonnegative a, c nonnegative; but if numerical -1e-18, max okay. We might clip c to 0? Score max; if tiny negative no. Use `np.maximum(c,0)`? If actual positive, clipping okay. But for gradients, c can be negative? Conv of nonnegative should nonneg; numerical negative tiny. Clip to 0 for LSE. In gradient derivative, v independent of c. Use clipped c for LSE. For exact score, max positive unaffected. Use `c[c<0]=0` to avoid LSE issues.
```
    c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
But if tiny negative becomes 0, sum slightly less than S^2? Not important. For normalized sum1, sum c may be <1 by tiny. In LSE, we don't rely sum. For project_box, we renormalize q. Good.

`score_info(a)` returns (score,c,cmax):
```
def score_info(a):
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-300: return np.inf, None, np.inf
    c=conv_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    sc=2.0*n*m/(S*S)
    return sc,c,m
```
If a normalized, S=1.

`grad_v_from_p(p,a)` as above:
```
def grad_v_from_p(p,a):
    n=a.size
    a_rev=a[::-1].copy()
    L=2*n-1 + n -1 # 3n-2
    N=next_pow2(L)
    Pp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    Ar=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,N)
    res=np.fft.irfft(Pp*Ar,N)[:L]
    return res[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length L = len(p)+len(a_rev)-1 = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2. Good.

`lse_z_value_grad(z,beta)`:
```
def lse_z_value_grad(z,beta):
    a = softmax_from_z(z)
    n=a.size
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(z)
    beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
    sh = beta*(c-cmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -700.0, 700.0)
    ex = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full_like(c, 1.0/len(c))
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = ex / s
        L = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    v = grad_v_from_p(p,a)
    g_a = 4.0 * n * v
    hat = float(np.dot(a, g_a))
    g_z = a * (g_a - hat)
    f = 2.0 * n * L
    return f, g_z
```
Is L formula if p uniform fallback? okay.

`lse_z_func(z,beta)`: returns tuple.

`run_lbfgs_z`:
```
def run_lbfgs_z(z0,beta,maxiter,maxfun,ftol=1e-14,gtol=1e-9):
    z0=np.asarray(z0,float).copy()
    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':ftol,'gtol':gtol,'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_z_func, z0, args=(float(beta),), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
       if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           return res.x
    except Exception as e:
       pass
    return z0
```
Need `lac=True`? We need keyword `jac=True`; yes.

Potential: `minimize` with `jac=True` expects objective function returns tuple; `lse_z_func` returns `(f,g)`. Good.

`lbfgs_lse_from_a(a, betas, maxiter_base, deadline, best_holder?)` Can't use closure; implement iteration in propose_candidate directly maybe easier. We'll write a function:
```
def run_lse_schedule(a, betas, maxiter_per_fun, deadline, best_score, best_a, label):
   best_score,... nonlocal not allowed in top-level function? We can return updated best_score,best_a,a_cur.
```
Top-level function can return tuple. Good.
```
def lse_schedule(a, betas, maxiter_base, deadline, best_score, best_a, verbose=True, label=''):
    a_cur=normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    cur_sc=score_info(a_cur)[0]
    if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_score:
       best_score=cur_sc; best_a=a_cur.copy()
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 15: break
       maxiter=max(50, int(rem*4))
       maxfun=max(maxiter*3, 200)
       z0=z_from_a(a_cur)
       z1=run_lbfgs_z(z0,beta,maxiter,maxfun)
       a1=softmax_from_z(z1)
       a1=normalize_sum1(a1)
       sc1=score_info(a1)[0]
       if verbose: print(...)
       if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
           best_score=sc1; best_a=a1.copy()
       if (not np.isfinite(cur_sc)) or sc1 <= cur_sc + 1e-8 or sc1 < best_score + 1e-12:

    return best_score,best_a,a_cur
```

```
def spectral_box_sequence(a, phase_deadline, best_score, best_a, target_score):
    a_work=normalize_sum1(a.copy()); best_score,best_a updated.
    n=a_work.size; N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    sc, c, m = score_info(a_work)
    cap_target = max(target_score/(2.0*n)*0.98, 1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)*0.9)
    cap = m*0.995
    it=0; no_imp=0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
       # decay cap
       cap = cap_target + 0.995*(cap - cap_target) if cap > cap_target else cap_target
       c = conv_fft(a_work)
       q = project_box(c, cap)
       a_new = spectral_sqrt_update(q, N, n)
       if a_new is None:
           break
       sc_new = score_info(a_new)[0]
       if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
           best_score=sc_new; best_a=a_new.copy(); no_imp=0
       else:
           no_imp += 1
       # if a_new not much worse, use it as work; else blend with best

           a_new = 0.3*best_a + 0.7*a_new; a_new=normalize_sum1(a_new)
       else:
           a_new = best_a.copy()
       a_work = a_new
       if no_imp > 500: break
       it +=1
       if it %200 ==0: print
    return best_score,best_a
```

```
def make_modes(n,m,rng,fmax):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    modes=[]
    lin=2*x-1; lin-=lin.mean(); ln=np.linalg.norm(lin); if ln>0: modes.append(lin/ln)
    quad=6*x*(1-x)-1; ...
    if len(modes)>=m: return np.asarray(modes[:m])
    k=0
    while len(modes)<m and k<10*m:
        f = int(1 + (fmax-1)*(len(modes)/max(1,m)))? maybe increasing
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
        md = np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + phase); center norm
        modes.append(md)
        md = np.sin(...); modes.append(md)
        k+=1

    M=np.asarray(modes)

    # ensure finite
    M=np.nan_to_num(M)
    return M[:m]
```

`fourier_lse_value_grad`:
```
def fourier_lse_value_grad(y, base, modes, beta):
    n=len(base)
    logbase = np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-300))
    u = logbase + np.asarray(modes,dtype=float).dot(np.asarray(y,float))
    a = softmax_from_z(u)
    c=conv_fft(a)
    ... same L,p,v
    g_a=4*n*v
    H=a*g_a
    hat=float(np.dot(H,a))
    # modes.T @ H: modes shape (m,n)
    gy = modes.T.dot(H) - hat * modes.T.dot(a)
    f=2*n*L
    return f,gy
```
Since modes center norm ~1, gradient scale fine.

```
def transfer_polish(a, phase_deadline, best_score, best_a, rng_seed=123):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    a=normalize_sum1(a.copy()); best updated
    n=a.size
    for _ in range(10000):
       if time>phase_deadline: break
       sc, c, m = score_info(a)
       # update if better (maybe after previous)
       # choose k_star = argmax
       k=int(np.argmax(c))
       jstart=max(0,k-n+1); jend=min(k,n-1)
       if jend<jstart: break
       js=np.arange(jstart,jend+1)
       contribs=a[js]*a[k-js]
       # top contributors
       ktop=min(8, contribs.size)
       top=np.argpartition(contribs, -ktop)[-ktop:] if ktop<contribs.size else np.arange(contribs.size)
       top_sorted=top[np.argsort(contribs[top])[::-1]]
       accepted=False
       for lt in top_sorted:
           j=int(jstart+lt)
           if a[j] <= 1e-12: continue
           # donor random sample
           donors = rng.integers(0,n,64)
           # choose donor with smallest a among those not too close? We can choose min a.
           d_idx=donors[np.argmin(a[donors])]
           if d_idx==j: continue
           # try fracs
           old=a.copy()
           frac_best=0; best_local=sc
           for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
               d=frac*a[j]
               cand=old.copy(); cand[j]=old[j]-d; cand[d_idx]+=d
               cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0); s=cand.sum(); if s>0: cand/=s
               sc_ans=score_info(cand)[0]
               if np.isfinite(sc_ans) and sc_ans < best_local:
                   best_local=sc_ans; frac_best=frac; best_cand=cand.copy()
           if frac_best>0:
               a=best_cand; sc=best_local; accepted=True
               if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
               break
       if not accepted:
           # maybe try move mass from top j to global low
           j=int(jstart+top_sorted[0])
           low_candidates=rng.integers(0,n,256)
           d_idx=low_candidates[np.argmin(a[low_candidates])]
           for frac in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
              d=frac*a[j]; cand=...; scans; if improve accept break
    return best_score,best_a
```

```
def transfer_polish(a, phase_deadline, best_score, best_a, rng_seed=999):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    a = normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    sc = best_score
    if a.size==0: return...
    best_sc,best_c,best_m=score_info(a)
    if best_sc<best_score: ...
    n=a.size
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<phase_deadline and it<3000:
       it +=1
       sc, c, _ = score_info(a)
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       # choose peak
       k=int(np.argmax(c))
       js...
       contribs...
       ktop=min(6, contribs.size)
       top=np.argpartition(contribs, -ktop)[-ktop:] if ktop<contribs.size else np.arange(contribs.size)
       top=top[np.argsort(contribs[top])[::-1]]
       for lt in top:
           j=...
           if a[j] < 1e-12: continue
           # choose donor from low quantile
           # Instead of random all, take 64 random, min
           donors = rng.integers(0,n,64)
           d_idx=int(donors[np.argmin(a[donors])])
           if d_idx==j: continue
           old=a
           best_local=sc; best_cand=None; best_frac=0
           for frac in (0.1,0.3,0.6):
               d=frac*a[j]
               cand=old.copy()
               cand[j] = old[j]-d
               cand[d_idx] += d
               s=cand.sum()
               if s<=0: continue
               cand = np.maximum(cand,0.0)
               cand = cand / cand.sum()
               sc_c=score_info(cand)[0]
               if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_local:
                   best_local=sc_c; best_cand=cand; best_frac=frac
           if best_cand is not None:
               a=best_cand; sc=best_local; break
       # optional if no improve for 200, perturb? maybe break
    return best_score,best_a
```

```python
"""
Improvement program for minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
for non-negative sequences of length 10000.

The program keeps the best sequence found and uses a large number of
complementary local/global optimizers:

- LSE-smoothed optimization with L-BFGS-B in log-softmax variables.
- Spectral square-root box-flattening iterations.
- Low-dimensional Fourier-mode searches, both from uniform and from the
  current best point.
- Full low-beta/high-beta restarts in log-space.
- Targeted transfer polish for the dominant autocorrelation peaks.

It reads a previous best from the global variable height_sequence_1 if
available, otherwise starts from a uniform sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
OUT_LEN = 10000


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s2


def resample_to_n(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, max(0.0, float(a[0])), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sum1(b)


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.zeros_like(z)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        z = np.zeros_like(z)
        zmax = 0.0
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones_like(z)
        s = float(z.size)
    return e / s


def z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(a)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return z - float(np.mean(z))


def conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_info(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, None, np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-300:
        return np.inf, None, np.inf
    c = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf, c, np.inf
    sc = 2.0 * float(n) * m / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return np.inf, c, m
    return sc, c, m


def grad_v_from_p(p, a):
    """
    v_j = sum_k p_k a_{k-j}, with linearity and zero out-of-range.
    This is the weighted derivative factor for the convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 3 * n - 2
    N = next_pow2(L)
    ar = a[::-1].copy()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    res = np.fft.irfft(P * A, N)[:L]
    res = np.nan_to_num(res, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return res[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def lse_z_value_grad(z, beta):
    a = softmax_from_z(z)
    n = a.size
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(z)

    beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
    sh = beta * (c - cmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(len(c), 1.0 / len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = e / s
        L = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    v = grad_v_from_p(p, a)
    g_a = 4.0 * float(n) * v
    hat = float(np.dot(a, g_a))
    g_z = a * (g_a - hat)
    f = 2.0 * float(n) * L
    f = float(f)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
        g_z = np.zeros_like(z)
    return f, g_z


def lse_z_func(z, beta):
    return lse_z_value_grad(z, beta)


def lse_z_value_only(z, beta):
    return lse_z_value_grad(z, beta)[0]


def lse_z_grad_only(z, beta):
    return lse_z_value_grad(z, beta)[1]


def run_lbfgs_z(z0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, ftol=1e-13, gtol=1e-9):
    z0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if z0.size == 0:
        return z0
    try:
        options = {
            'maxiter': int(max(maxiter, 1)),
            'maxfun': int(max(maxfun, 2 * maxiter + 10)),
            'ftol': ftol,
            'gtol': gtol,
            'maxls': 40
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(lse_z_func, z0, args=(float(beta),),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
        except Exception:
            res = minimize(lse_z_value_only, z0, args=(float(beta),),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', jac=lse_z_grad_only,
                           options=options)
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x'):
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return z0


def lbfgs_lse_one(a, beta, maxiter, maxfun):
    a = normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    z0 = z_from_a(a)
    z1 = run_lbfgs_z(z0, beta, maxiter, maxfun)
    a1 = normalize_sum1(softmax_from_z(z1))
    sc = score_info(a1)[0]
    return a1, sc


def project_box(q, cap):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        q = np.full(len(q), 1.0 / max(1, len(q)), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = q / s
    cap = max(float(cap), 1.0 / float(len(q)) * (1.0 + 1e-12))

    for _ in range(30):
        over = q > cap
        if not np.any(over):
            break
        excess = float((q[over] - cap).sum())
        q[over] = cap
        under = q < cap - 1e-15
        if np.any(under):
            q[under] += excess / float(np.count_nonzero(under))
        else:
            q += excess / float(len(q))

    s = float(q.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        q = q / s
    return q


def spectral_sqrt_update(q, N, n):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    L = min(len(q), N)
    c[:L] = q
    Q = np.fft.fft(c)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(Q.real)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(Q.imag)):
        return None

    A = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.complex128)
    half = N // 2
    A[:half + 1] = np.sqrt(Q[:half + 1])
    if N % 2 == 0:
        A[half] = complex(np.sqrt(max(0.0, Q[half].real)), 0.0)
    for k in range(1, half):
        A[N - k] = np.conj(A[k])

    x = np.fft.ifft(A)[:n]
    x = np.real(x)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return x / s


def spectral_box_sequence(a, phase_deadline, best_score, best_a, target_score):
    a_work = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy())
    n = a_work.size
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    sc, _, m = score_info(a_work)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_a = a_work.copy()

    min_cap = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
    cap_target = max(target_score / (2.0 * n) * 0.985, min_cap * 1.05)
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        cap = cap_target
    else:
        cap = max(cap_target, m * 0.995)

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
        it += 1
        if cap > cap_target:
            cap = cap_target + 0.997 * (cap - cap_target)
        else:
            cap = cap_target

        c = conv_fft(a_work)
        q = project_box(c, cap)
        a_new = spectral_sqrt_update(q, N, n)
        if a_new is None:
            break

        sc_new = score_info(a_new)[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        sc_cur, _, _ = score_info(a_work)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
            a_work = best_a.copy()
        elif sc_new <= best_score + 0.02:
            a_work = 0.45 * a_work + 0.55 * a_new
            a_work = normalize_sum1(a_work)
        elif sc_new <= best_score + 0.10:
            a_work = 0.80 * a_work + 0.20 * a_new
            a_work = normalize_sum1(a_work)
        else:
            a_work = best_a.copy()

        if no_improve > 700:
            a_work = best_a.copy()
            _, _, m = score_info(a_work)
            if np.isfinite(m) and m > cap_target:
                cap = max(cap_target, m * 0.995)
            no_improve = 0

        if it % 250 == 0:
            print(f"  spectral it={it} best={best_score:.8f} cap={cap:.8e}")

    return best_score, best_a


def make_modes(n, m, rng, fmax):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    modes = []

    trend = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    trend -= float(np.mean(trend))
    tn = float(np.linalg.norm(trend))
    if tn > 1e-12:
        modes.append(trend / tn)

    hump = 6.0 * x * (1.0 - x) - 1.0
    hump -= float(np.mean(hump))
    hn = float(np.linalg.norm(hump))
    if hn > 1e-12:
        modes.append(hump / hn)

    k = 0
    fmax = int(max(2, min(int(fmax), 500)))
    while len(modes) < m and k < 400:
        frac = k / float(max(1, 200))
        f = int(1 + (fmax - 1) * frac)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        md = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
        md -= float(np.mean(md))
        mn = float(np.linalg.norm(md))
        if mn > 1e-12:
            modes.append(md / mn)
        if len(modes) < m:
            sd = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
            sd -= float(np.mean(sd))
            sn = float(np.linalg.norm(sd))
            if sn > 1e-12:
                modes.append(sd / sn)
        k += 1

    M = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    mm = min(m, len(modes))
    if mm > 0:
        M[:mm, :] = np.asarray(modes[:mm], dtype=np.float64)
    return np.nan_to_num(M, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def fourier_lse_value_grad(y, logbase, modes, beta):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    u = np.asarray(logbase, dtype=np.float64).ravel() + np.asarray(modes, dtype=np.float64).dot(y)
    a = softmax_from_z(u)
    n = a.size
    c = conv_fft(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(y)

    beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
    sh = beta * (c - cmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(len(c), 1.0 / len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = e / s
        L = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    v = grad_v_from_p(p, a)
    g_a = 4.0 * float(n) * v
    H = a * g_a
    hat = float(np.dot(H, a))
    modes_arr = np.asarray(modes, dtype=np.float64)
    gy = modes_arr.T.dot(H) - hat * modes_arr.T.dot(a)
    f = 2.0 * float(n) * L
    f = float(f)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
        gy = np.zeros_like(y)
    return f, gy


def fourier_lse_func(y, logbase, modes, beta):
    return fourier_lse_value_grad(y, logbase, modes, beta)


def fourier_lse_value_only(y, logbase, modes, beta):
    return fourier_lse_value_grad(y, logbase, modes, beta)[0]


def fourier_lse_grad_only(y, logbase, modes, beta):
    return fourier_lse_value_grad(y, logbase, modes, beta)[1]


def lowdim_fourier_search(base, m, beta, bound, maxiter, fmax, seed, random_scale=0.0):
    base = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy())
    n = base.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    modes = make_modes(n, m, rng, fmax)
    logbase = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-300))

    y0 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if random_scale > 0.0:
        y0 = float(random_scale) * rng.standard_normal(m)
        y0 = np.clip(y0, -float(bound), float(bound))

    bounds = [(-float(bound), float(bound))] * m
    options = {
        'maxiter': int(max(maxiter, 10)),
        'maxfun': int(max(200, 4 * maxiter)),
        'ftol': 1e-13,
        'gtol': 1e-8,
        'maxls': 30
    }

    try:
        try:
            res = minimize(fourier_lse_func, y0,
                           args=(logbase, modes, float(beta)),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True,
                           options=options)
        except Exception:
            res = minimize(fourier_lse_value_only, y0,
                           args=(logbase, modes, float(beta)),
                           method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                           jac=fourier_lse_grad_only, options=options)
        y1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        y1 = np.clip(y1, -float(bound), float(bound))
        u = logbase + modes.dot(y1)
        a1 = normalize_sum1(softmax_from_z(u))
        return a1
    except Exception:
        return base.copy()


def transfer_polish(a, phase_deadline, best_score, best_a, rng_seed=12345):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    a = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy())
    sc, _, _ = score_info(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_a = a.copy()

    n = a.size
    it = 0
    stagnant = 0

    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline and it < 4000 and stagnant < 800:
        it += 1
        sc, c, _ = score_info(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)
        if j1 < j0:
            break
        js = np.arange(j0, j1 + 1)
        contrib = a[js] * a[k - js]
        ktop = min(6, contrib.size)
        if ktop <= 0:
            break
        if ktop < contrib.size:
            top = np.argpartition(contrib, -ktop)[-ktop:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(contrib.size)
        top = top[np.argsort(contrib[top])[::-1]]

        improved = False
        for lt in top:
            j = int(j0 + int(lt))
            if a[j] < 1e-12:
                continue
            donors = rng.integers(0, n, 64)
            d = int(donors[np.argmin(a[donors])])
            if d == j:
                continue
            best_local = sc
            best_cand = None
            for frac in (0.08, 0.22, 0.45):
                delta = frac * a[j]
                cand = a.copy()
                cand[j] -= delta
                cand[d] += delta
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 0:
                    cand = normalize_sum1(a)
                else:
                    cand = cand / s
                sc_c = score_info(cand)[0]
                if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_local - 1e-15:
                    best_local = sc_c
                    best_cand = cand.copy()
            if best_cand is not None:
                a = best_cand
                sc = best_local
                improved = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if not improved:
            stagnant += 1
        else:
            stagnant = 0

    return best_score, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, min(float(budget_s), 2400.0))
    hard_deadline = start + budget - 60.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None
    n = OUT_LEN

    def update_best(a, label=""):
        nonlocal best_score, best_a
        if a is None:
            return
        a = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        sc = score_info(a)[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            print(f"  NEW BEST [{label}]: {sc:.8f} n={a.size} t={time.monotonic()-start:.0f}s")

    try:
        parent = None
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            arr = np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 64:
                arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if arr.sum() > 0:
                    parent = arr
        if parent is not None:
            if parent.size != n:
                parent = resample_to_n(parent, n)
            parent = normalize_sum1(parent)
            update_best(parent, "parent")
            print(f"loaded parent n={parent.size} score={best_score:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        update_best(a0, "uniform")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    print(f"initial best={best_score:.8f}, n={best_a.size}, budget={budget:.0f}s")

    # Phase 1: quick LSE annealing directly from the current best point.
    ph1_end = start + 0.14 * (budget - 60.0)
    lse_a = best_a.copy()
    lse_sc = best_score
    for beta, mi in [(800, 260), (3000, 320), (10000, 400), (30000, 420)]:
        if time.monotonic() > ph1_end - 10:
            break
        if lse_sc > best_score + 1e-8:
            lse_a = best_a.copy()
            lse_sc = best_score
        a_try, sc_try = lbfgs_lse_one(lse_a, beta, mi, 3 * mi + 100)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            print(f"  LSE beta={beta}: {sc_try:.8f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= lse_sc + 1e-8:
            lse_a = a_try
            lse_sc = sc_try

    # Phase 2: spectral square-root box flattening.
    ph2_end = start + 0.34 * (budget - 60.0)
    best_score, best_a = spectral_box_sequence(best_a, ph2_end, best_score, best_a, TARGET_SCORE)

    # Phase 3: low-dimensional Fourier searches from scratch.
    ph3_end = start + 0.50 * (budget - 60.0)
    a_uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    scratch_settings = [
        (50, 1200, 1.4, 34, 420, 0.0, 11),
        (90, 4000, 2.0, 42, 460, 0.45, 22),
        (180, 12000, 2.6, 50, 520, 0.70, 33),
        (320, 25000, 3.0, 58, 420, 0.95, 44)
    ]
    for fmax, beta, bound, m, mi, rscale, sd in scratch_settings:
        if time.monotonic() > ph3_end - 20:
            break
        a_try = lowdim_fourier_search(a_uni, m, beta, bound, mi, fmax, sd, rscale)
        sc_try = score_info(a_try)[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            print(f"  lowdim scratch fmax={fmax} beta={beta}: {sc_try:.8f}")
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    # Phase 4: low-dimensional Fourier perturbations around the current best.
    ph4_end = start + 0.62 * (budget - 60.0)
    center_settings = [
        (80, 10000, 0.75, 34, 360, 121),
        (220, 20000, 1.00, 46, 400, 202),
        (420, 35000, 1.25, 54, 320, 303)
    ]
    for fmax, beta, bound, m, mi, sd in center_settings:
        if time.monotonic() > ph4_end - 20:
            break
        a_try = lowdim_fourier_search(best_a, m, beta, bound, mi, fmax, sd, 0.0)
        sc_try = score_info(a_try)[0]
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            print(f"  lowdim center fmax={fmax} beta={beta}: {sc_try:.8f}")
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    # Phase 5: full restarts in log-space.
    ph5_end = start + 0.80 * (budget - 60.0)
    perturb_modes = make_modes(n, 42, rng, 240)
    for r in range(70):
        if time.monotonic() > ph5_end - 15:
            break
        z0 = z_from_a(best_a)
        scale = 0.40 / (1.0 + 0.07 * r)
        w = scale * rng.standard_normal(perturb_modes.shape[0])
        z1 = z0 + perturb_modes.dot(w)
        a_pert = normalize_sum1(softmax_from_z(z1))
        beta = 8000 + ((r % 5) * 4000)
        mi = 220
        a_try, sc_try = lbfgs_lse_one(a_pert, beta, mi, 3 * mi + 100)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            print(f"  restart {r} beta={beta}: {sc_try:.8f}")
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    # Phase 6: targeted transfer polish.
    ph6_end = start + 0.90 * (budget - 60.0)
    best_score, best_a = transfer_polish(best_a, ph6_end, best_score, best_a, rng_seed=int(rng.integers(0, 10**9)))

    # Phase 7: final high-beta LSE polish.
    final_settings = [(12000, 420), (40000, 360), (90000, 260)]
    for beta, mi in final_settings:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 20:
            break
        a_try, sc_try = lbfgs_lse_one(best_a, beta, mi, 3 * mi + 100)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
            print(f"  final LSE beta={beta}: {sc_try:.8f}")
            best_score = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    # Ensure correct output length and clean values.
    best_a = resample_to_n(best_a, OUT_LEN)
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) <= 0.01:
        out = [1.0 / 10000.0] * OUT_LEN

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal best = {best_score:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified     = {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"length             = {len(out)}")
    print(f"runtime            = {time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s / {budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
